DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player taps to place a short‑range portal ahead of a constantly moving dot; when the dot reaches the portal it instantly jumps forward, skipping any obstacles in between, with a cooldown that forces strategic timing.

## Player
A small glowing dot that automatically travels forward. The player can **place** a portal by tapping/clicking, which creates a temporary gateway a short distance ahead. The dot also **moves** forward continuously and **waits** while the portal cooldown is active.

## Core loop
Obstacles stream toward the dot as it advances. The player watches the upcoming section, decides whether to tap and create a portal, then watches the dot either collide with obstacles or enter the portal and teleport forward. After each portal use a cooldown period begins, during which the player must navigate the remaining hazards until the cooldown expires and another portal can be placed.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Static spikes** that sit on the path and instantly kill the dot on contact.
- **Moving sweepers** that slide horizontally or vertically, their motion telegraphed by a glowing trail.
- **Rotating lasers** that pivot around a fixed point, creating a rotating danger zone.
- **Falling blocks** that drop from the top, landing in the dot’s lane after a short delay.
Each hazard occupies a segment of the lane and can be bypassed only by timing a portal correctly.

## Score
Score increments each time the dot successfully avoids an obstacle, whether by passing it normally or by teleporting past it with a portal. Additional points are awarded for each portal use that skips multiple hazards in one jump.

## Win and lose
The player loses when the dot contacts any hazard while the portal is unavailable. The player wins by reaching a predefined distance or surviving a set number of obstacle waves, at which point a celebratory overlay appears.

## Feel
Frantic and high‑energy, with a neon‑lit aesthetic that glows against a dark background. The soundtrack consists of rapid chiptune arpeggios and short beeps marking portal placements and collisions, reinforcing the fast‑paced, risky feel.

## Why it's fun
The thrill of perfectly timing a portal to zip past a cluster of dangers gives an instant, satisfying “whoosh” moment that rewards quick reflexes and strategic foresight.

TASKS.md

show
1. HTML scaffold + meta.json — create index.html with required header, canvas, restart button, empty script block and matching meta.json per CONVENTIONS.md and concept.
2. Game state objects — define player, hazards array, score, phase/state as plain objects at top of script with initial values.
3. Animation loop — implement requestAnimationFrame loop with startLoop, cancelAnimationFrame discipline, dt calculation, try/catch, clear canvas.
4. Input handling — set up keys Set for keydown/keyup, R-key restart, restart button click, shared reset() function.
5. Player movement — dt‑based forward motion, apply acceleration, handle boundaries, update player position each frame.
6. Portal creation & cooldown — tap/click creates short‑range portal ahead of player, starts cooldown timer, teleports player when reaching portal.
7. Hazard system — define hazard data structure, spawn rules respecting SAFE_RADIUS (no spawn within safe radius or reaching player within 3 s), dt‑scaled motion.
8. Collision detection — detect player vs hazards, trigger game over state and stop gameplay loop.
9. Scoring — increase score on each avoided hazard, extra points for portal skips, draw score on canvas top‑left.
10. Rendering — clear canvas, draw background, hazards, portal, player (high‑contrast), HUD elements.
11. Game‑over overlay — dim background, show "Game Over", final score, and restart instructions.
12. Observable contract — populate window.__game each frame with state, score, player.x/y/visible, and objective sentence.
13. Audio — lazy‑init AudioContext in beep() helper, wrap in try/catch, hook into hit, score, portal, and death events.

<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Portal Skip</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Tap to create a short‑range portal ahead of the dot; when the dot reaches the portal it instantly teleports forward, skipping any obstacles in between, with a cooldown limiting how often it can be used.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Space to create portal</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
    // === CONFIG =============================================================
    // Game constants
    const PLAYER_SCREEN_X = 100; // screen X position of player dot
    const PLAYER_Y = 540; // vertical position near bottom
    const PLAYER_RADIUS = 8;
    const PLAYER_SPEED_BASE = 150; // px/sec
    const PLAYER_MAX_SPEED = 300;
    const PLAYER_ACCEL = 20; // px/sec^2
    const PORTAL_AHEAD_DISTANCE = 80; // distance ahead of player for portal creation
    const PORTAL_SKIP_DISTANCE = 200; // distance player jumps when portal reached
    const PORTAL_COOLDOWN = 1.5; // seconds
    const PORTAL_RADIUS = 10;
    const PORTAL_SKIP_BONUS = 5; // extra points for portal skip
    const HAZARD_W = 20;
    const HAZARD_H = 20;
    const HAZARD_SPAWN_INTERVAL = 1.0; // seconds between spawns
    const SAFE_RADIUS = 200; // no spawn within this distance of player
    

    // === STATE ==============================================================
    const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

    let player;
    let hazards;
    let score;
    let phase;
    let keys;
    let lastTime;
    let loopId = null;
    let audioCtx = null;

    // === RESET ==============================================================
    function reset() {
      // Initialize game state
      player = { x: canvas.width / 2, y: canvas.height - 30 };
      hazards = []; // empty array
      score = 0;
      phase = "playing";
      keys = new Set();
      lastTime = performance.now();

      startLoop();
    }

    // === LOOP ==============================================================
    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;

          // Update game state and render
          update(dt);
          render();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("loop error", err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    // === UPDATE ==============================================================
function update(dt) {
  if (phase !== "playing") return;
  // Increase score over time (seconds)
  score += dt;
}

// === RENDER ==============================================================
function render() {
  // Clear background
  ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  // Draw player
  ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, PLAYER_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  // Draw score
  ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
  ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.fillText(`Score ${Math.floor(score)}`, 12, 22);
}

// === INPUT ==============================================================
    document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
      if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") {
        reset();
        return;
      }
      keys.add(ev.key);
    });
    document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => {
      keys.delete(ev.key);
    });
    restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

    // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
function beep(freq, durSec) {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.type = "square";
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
  } catch (e) {
    // ignore errors
  }
}

// === BOOT ==============================================================
reset();
    reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

prompt (13,905 chars)
## Reference example: "breakout"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

### Example `meta.json`
```json
{
  "title": "Neon Breaker",
  "description": "Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.",
  "controls": [
    "Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle",
    "Hold Space to launch faster",
    "Press R or click Restart"
  ],
  "tags": ["arcade", "breakout", "bouncing"]
}
```

### Example `index.html`
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Neon Breaker</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #0f1320; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Breaker</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle</li>
      <li>Hold Space to launch faster</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const PADDLE_W = 110;
  const PADDLE_H = 14;
  const PADDLE_Y = 560;
  const PADDLE_SPEED = 700;       // px/sec

  const BALL_R = 7;
  const BALL_BASE_SPEED = 360;    // px/sec at start
  const BALL_MAX_SPEED  = 720;
  const BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT = 6; // px/sec added per brick

  const BRICK_ROWS = 6;
  const BRICK_COLS = 12;
  const BRICK_W = 60;
  const BRICK_H = 20;
  const BRICK_GAP = 4;
  const BRICK_TOP = 60;
  const BRICK_LEFT = (800 - (BRICK_COLS * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP) - BRICK_GAP)) / 2;
  const ROW_COLORS = ["#ff52ae", "#ff8c52", "#ffd23f", "#a3ff52", "#52ffae", "#7df9ff"];

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let paddle;        // { x } center x
  let ball;          // { x, y, vx, vy, speed }
  let bricks;        // array of { x, y, w, h, color, alive }
  let score;
  let bricksLeft;
  let phase;         // "playing" | "won" | "lost"
  let keys;          // pressed key set
  let highScore = 0;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    paddle = { x: 400 };
    ball = { x: 400, y: 480, vx: 0, vy: 0, speed: BALL_BASE_SPEED };
    launchBall();
    bricks = [];
    for (let r = 0; r < BRICK_ROWS; r++) {
      for (let c = 0; c < BRICK_COLS; c++) {
        bricks.push({
          x: BRICK_LEFT + c * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP),
          y: BRICK_TOP + r * (BRICK_H + BRICK_GAP),
          w: BRICK_W, h: BRICK_H,
          color: ROW_COLORS[r % ROW_COLORS.length],
          alive: true,
        });
      }
    }
    bricksLeft = bricks.length;
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function launchBall() {
    // Always launch upward, mild horizontal lean either side.
    const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + (Math.random() * 0.6 - 0.3);
    ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
    ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Paddle.
    const dirX = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
               - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
    const speedMul = keys.has(" ") ? 1.6 : 1;
    paddle.x += dirX * PADDLE_SPEED * speedMul * dt;
    paddle.x = clamp(paddle.x, PADDLE_W / 2, canvas.width - PADDLE_W / 2);

    // Ball motion.
    ball.x += ball.vx * dt;
    ball.y += ball.vy * dt;

    // Walls.
    if (ball.x < BALL_R) { ball.x = BALL_R; ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.x > canvas.width - BALL_R) { ball.x = canvas.width - BALL_R; ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.y < BALL_R) { ball.y = BALL_R; ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy); beep(220, 0.04); }

    // Floor — lose.
    if (ball.y > canvas.height + BALL_R) {
      phase = "lost";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(110, 0.25);
      return;
    }

    // Paddle.
    const px = paddle.x, py = PADDLE_Y;
    if (ball.vy > 0
        && ball.y + BALL_R >= py
        && ball.y - BALL_R <= py + PADDLE_H
        && ball.x >= px - PADDLE_W / 2 - BALL_R
        && ball.x <= px + PADDLE_W / 2 + BALL_R) {
      // Bounce direction depends on where it hit on the paddle.
      const offset = (ball.x - px) / (PADDLE_W / 2); // -1..1
      const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + offset * 1.0;   // up-ish, leaning side
      ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.y = py - BALL_R - 0.1;
      beep(540, 0.05);
    }

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      if (ball.x + BALL_R < b.x || ball.x - BALL_R > b.x + b.w) continue;
      if (ball.y + BALL_R < b.y || ball.y - BALL_R > b.y + b.h) continue;

      b.alive = false;
      bricksLeft--;
      score += 10;
      ball.speed = Math.min(BALL_MAX_SPEED, ball.speed + BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT);

      // Decide which side was hit by comparing penetrations.
      const overlapX = Math.min(ball.x + BALL_R - b.x, b.x + b.w - (ball.x - BALL_R));
      const overlapY = Math.min(ball.y + BALL_R - b.y, b.y + b.h - (ball.y - BALL_R));
      if (overlapX < overlapY) ball.vx = -ball.vx; else ball.vy = -ball.vy;

      // Renormalize to current speed.
      const m = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
      ball.vx = (ball.vx / m) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = (ball.vy / m) * ball.speed;
      beep(880, 0.05);
      break;
    }

    if (bricksLeft === 0) {
      phase = "won";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(990, 0.2);
    }
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      ctx.fillStyle = b.color;
      ctx.fillRect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h);
    }

    // Paddle.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.fillRect(paddle.x - PADDLE_W / 2, PADDLE_Y, PADDLE_W, PADDLE_H);

    // Ball.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, BALL_R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Bricks ${bricksLeft}  ·  Best ${highScore}`, canvas.width - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "lost" || phase === "won") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,15,0.7)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = phase === "won" ? "#52ffae" : "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText(phase === "won" ? "You Win!" : "Game Over", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "square";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase === "playing" ? "playing" : "gameover",
      score,
      player: {
        x: paddle.x,
        y: PADDLE_Y + PADDLE_H / 2,
        visible: paddle.x >= 0 && paddle.x <= canvas.width,
      },
      objective: "Bounce the ball with the paddle to break every brick.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function clamp(v, lo, hi) { return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

Let's construct script:

```js
// === CONFIG =============================================================
// (none needed now)

// === STATE ==============================================================
const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

let player;
let hazards;
let score;
let phase;
let keys;
let lastTime;
let loopId = null;
let audioCtx = null;

// === RESET ==============================================================
function reset() {
  // Initialize game state
  player = { x: canvas.width / 2, y: canvas.height - 30 };
  hazards = []; // empty array
  score = 0;
  phase = "playing";
  keys = new Set();
  lastTime = performance.now();

  startLoop();
}

// === LOOP ==============================================================
function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;

      // Clear canvas
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("loop error", err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

// === INPUT ==============================================================
document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
  if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") {
    reset();
    return;
  }
  keys.add(ev.key);
});
document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => {
  keys.delete(ev.key);
});
restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

// === BOOT ==============================================================
reset();
```

We may need to ensure variable 'keys' exists before using in event listener. It's defined later but in JS, function closure will capture after defined; but keydown listener runs after script loads (so keys defined later). However event listener is defined before keys initialization (keys variable is hoisted as let not defined yet). In the code order above keys is defined as let keys; at top of script; it's before event listener definitions (since we defined keys earlier). The code order: after RESET we define startLoop, then after that define event listeners. At that point keys has been declared but not initialized; inside event listeners we use keys.add ... keys may still be undefined at time of keydown if reset hasn't set it yet, but reset is called at Boot after event listeners are added. So keys will be set when the first keydown comes after resetting - the boot call to reset runs before any user input. So fine.

```js
    // === CONFIG =============================================================
    // (none needed now)

    // === STATE ==============================================================
    const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

    let player;
    let hazards;
    let score;
    let phase;
    let keys;
    let lastTime;
    let loopId = null;
    let audioCtx = null;

    // === RESET ==============================================================
    function reset() {
      // Initialize game state
      player = { x: canvas.width / 2, y: canvas.height - 30 };
      hazards = []; // empty array
      score = 0;
      phase = "playing";
      keys = new Set();
      lastTime = performance.now();

      startLoop();
    }

    // === LOOP ==============================================================
    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;

          // Clear canvas
          ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("loop error", err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    // === INPUT ==============================================================
    document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
      if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") {
        reset();
        return;
      }
      keys.add(ev.key);
    });
    document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => {
      keys.delete(ev.key);
    });
    restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

    // === BOOT ==============================================================
    reset();
```

Make sure each line starts with 4 spaces.

Lines 62-78:

```
    // === LOOP ==============================================================
    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;

          // Clear canvas
          ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("loop error", err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
```

We will replace this block with:

```
// === UPDATE ==============================================================
    function update(dt) {
      // Player acceleration and forward motion
      player.speed = Math.min(PLAYER_MAX_SPEED, player.speed + PLAYER_ACCEL * dt);
      player.worldX += player.speed * dt;

      // Portal cooldown
      if (portalCooldown > 0) portalCooldown -= dt;

      // Spawn hazards
      spawnTimer -= dt;
      if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
        spawnHazard();
        spawnTimer = HAZARD_SPAWN_INTERVAL;
      }

      // Update hazards: check for collision and passing
      for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const h = hazards[i];
        // Compute on-screen X position
        const screenX = h.x - player.worldX + PLAYER_SCREEN_X;
        // Off left side: avoided
        if (screenX + h.w < 0) {
          hazards.splice(i, 1);
          score += 1; // avoided hazard
          continue;
        }
        // Collision check
        if (phase === "playing" && screenX < PLAYER_SCREEN_X + PLAYER_RADIUS && screenX + h.w > PLAYER_SCREEN_X - PLAYER_RADIUS) {
          // Simple collision on x; y aligned
          phase = "gameover";
        }
      }

      // Portal logic
      if (portal && phase === "playing") {
        const portalScreenX = portal.worldX - player.worldX + PLAYER_SCREEN_X;
        // Player reached portal
        if (player.worldX >= portal.worldX) {
          // Teleport forward
          player.worldX += PORTAL_SKIP_DISTANCE;
          // Give extra points
          score += PORTAL_SKIP_BONUS;
          // Remove portal
          portal = null;
          // Start cooldown
          portalCooldown = PORTAL_COOLDOWN;
        }
      }
    }

    function render() {
      // Background
      ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

      // Draw hazards
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ff5252";
      for (const h of hazards) {
        const screenX = h.x - player.worldX + PLAYER_SCREEN_X;
        ctx.fillRect(screenX, h.y, h.w, h.h);
      }

      // Draw portal
      if (portal) {
        const portalScreenX = portal.worldX - player.worldX + PLAYER_SCREEN_X;
        ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(portalScreenX + PORTAL_RADIUS, PLAYER_Y, PORTAL_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }

      // Draw player
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(PLAYER_SCREEN_X, PLAYER_Y, PLAYER_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();

      // Draw score
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.textAlign = "left";
      ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    }

    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;
          update(dt);
          render();
          // publishState() will be called later
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("loop error", err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
```

    function startLoop() {

          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;

```

We will replace it with:

```
    // === UPDATE ==============================================================
    // Update logic
    function update(dt) {
      // Player acceleration and forward motion
      player.speed = Math.min(PLAYER_MAX_SPEED, player.speed + PLAYER_ACCEL * dt);
      player.worldX += player.speed * dt;

      // Portal cooldown timer
      if (portalCooldown > 0) portalCooldown -= dt;

      // Hazard spawning
      spawnTimer -= dt;
      if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
        // Ensure safe spawn distance
        const minX = player.worldX + SAFE_RADIUS + PLAYER_SPEED_BASE * 3;
        const spawnX = minX + Math.random() * 200;
        hazards.push({
          x: spawnX,
          y: PLAYER_Y - HAZARD_H / 2,
          w: HAZARD_W,
          h: HAZARD_H,
        });
        spawnTimer = HAZARD_SPAWN_INTERVAL;
      }

      // Update hazards: collision and passing
      for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const h = hazards[i];
        const screenX = h.x - player.worldX + PLAYER_SCREEN_X;
        // Off-screen left: avoided
        if (screenX + h.w < 0) {
          hazards.splice(i, 1);
          score += 1; // avoided hazard
          continue;
        }
        // Collision detection (player and hazard overlap)
        if (phase === "playing" && screenX < PLAYER_SCREEN_X + PLAYER_RADIUS && screenX + h.w > PLAYER_SCREEN_X - PLAYER_RADIUS) {
          phase = "gameover";
        }
      }

      // Portal logic
      if (portal && phase === "playing") {
        // Player reached portal world position
        if (player.worldX >= portal.worldX) {
          // Teleport forward
          player.worldX += PORTAL_SKIP_DISTANCE;
          // Give extra points
          score += PORTAL_SKIP_BONUS;
          // Clear portal and start cooldown
          portal = null;
          portalCooldown = PORTAL_COOLDOWN;
        }
      }
    }

    // Render logic
    function render() {
      // Clear background
      ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

      // Draw hazards
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ff5252";
      for (const h of hazards) {
        const screenX = h.x - player.worldX + PLAYER_SCREEN_X;
        ctx.fillRect(screenX, h.y, h.w, h.h);
      }

      // Draw portal
      if (portal) {
        const portalScreenX = portal.worldX - player.worldX + PLAYER_SCREEN_X;
        ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(portalScreenX + PORTAL_RADIUS, PLAYER_Y, PORTAL_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }

      // Draw player
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(PLAYER_SCREEN_X, PLAYER_Y, PLAYER_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();

      // Draw score
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.textAlign = "left";
      ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    }

    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;
          update(dt);
          render();
          // publishState() will be called later
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("loop error", err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
```

lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);\n          lastTime = now;\n          update(dt);\n          render();\n          // publishState() will be called later\n        } catch (err) {\n          console.error(\"loop error\", err);\n        }\n        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n      }\n      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n    }\n```\n\nBut we must ensure we define constants like PLAYER_MAX_SPEED, PLAYER_ACCEL, PLAYER_SCREEN_X, PLAYER_Y, PLAYER_RADIUS, PORTAL_RADIUS, PORTAL_SKIP_DISTANCE, PORTAL_SKIP_BONUS, PORTAL_COOLDOWN, HAZARD_SPAWN_INTERVAL, HAZARD_W, HAZARD_H, etc, and also variables portalCooldown, spawnTimer.\n\nDefine them in CONFIG block.\n\nWe also need spawnHazard() function. ```js
// === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
function beep(freq, durSec) {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.type = "square";
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
  } catch (e) {
    // Silent fail
  }
}

// === BOOT ==============================================================
reset();
```

// === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
function beep(freq, durSec) {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.type = "square";
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
  } catch (e) {
    // ignore errors
  }
}

// === BOOT ==============================================================
reset();
```

prompt (5,163 chars)
Read CONVENTIONS.md and DESIGN.md in your working directory before doing anything else.

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Portal Skip
Concept: Tap to create a short‑range portal ahead of the dot; when the dot reaches the portal it instantly teleports forward, skipping any obstacles in between, with a cooldown limiting how often it can be used.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file.

**Tool calls MUST be emitted as actual function/tool invocations — NOT described or simulated inside your reasoning/analysis text.** Writing tool-shaped JSON like `{"filePath":"TASKS.md","content":"..."}` *inside your chain-of-thought* does NOT invoke any tool. It is just text in the analysis channel that nothing reads or executes. The same goes for `{"pattern":"**/*"}` etc. — those only do something when emitted as a real `tool_use` event from the action channel, never when written into your thinking. Several prior runs have failed by hallucinating tool calls inside reasoning; do not repeat that pattern. If at any point you "describe" a tool call in your thinking instead of invoking it, you must immediately invoke the real tool afterward. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called your write/edit tool with `TASKS.md` as the path. If you only described the task list in chat without invoking the tool, the file will not exist on disk and validation will fail. After the file is written, stop. Do not begin implementation.

Per-frame constants (`x += 5` every frame) make games run 2.4× faster on a 144Hz display.\n123: \n124: ```js\n125: let lastTime = performance.now();\n126: function loop(now) {\n127:   const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1); // seconds, clamped\n128:   lastTime = now;\n129:   // physics scaled by dt:\n130:   player.x += player.vx * dt;\n131:   enemy.y  += enemy.vy  * dt;\n132:   spawnTimer -= dt;\n133:   // ...\n134:   requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n135: }\n136: requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n137: ```\n138: \n139: ### 6.
